integrate_rule_total_correct
     : forall (r : rule_order) (f : float -> float) (b : block) (fd : fundef),
       Genv.find_funct ge (Vptr b Ptrofs.zero) = Some fd ->
       type_of_fundef fd =
       Tfunction [Tfloat F64 noattr] (Tfloat F64 noattr) cc_default ->
       (forall i : nat,
        (i < rule_size r)%nat ->
        Clight2.eval_funcall ge initial_memory fd [
          Vfloat (rule_node r i)] E0 initial_memory
          (Vfloat (f (rule_node r i)))) ->
       total_call_correct ge
         (Callstate (Internal f_integrate)
            [Vptr b Ptrofs.zero; Vint (Int.repr (Z.of_nat (rule_size r)))]
            Kstop initial_memory) (Vfloat (rule_value f r)) initial_memory

  (forall n : nat, {P n} + {~ P n}) ->
  {n : nat | ~ P n} + {forall n : nat, P n}
Axioms.proof_irr : ClassicalFacts.proof_irrelevance
inline_assembly_sem : String.string -> AST.signature -> extcall_sem
inline_assembly_properties :
  forall (id : String.string) (sg : AST.signature),
  extcall_properties (inline_assembly_sem id sg) sg
FunctionalExtensionality.functional_extensionality_dep :
  forall (A : Type) (B : A -> Type) (f g : forall x : A, B x),
  (forall x : A, f x = g x) -> f = g
external_functions_sem : String.string -> AST.signature -> extcall_sem
external_functions_properties :
  forall (id : String.string) (sg : AST.signature),
  extcall_properties (external_functions_sem id sg) sg
Eqdep.Eq_rect_eq.eq_rect_eq :
  forall (U : Type) (p : U) (Q : U -> Type) (x : Q p) (h : p = p),
  x = eq_rect p Q x p h
Classical_Prop.classic : forall P : Prop, P \/ ~ P
